Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on genomics structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 980. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 924 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to genomics theory, we decompose the problem into 980 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 924. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
